function tacticCoachPresets(){return Array.isArray(window.ML_COACH_TACTICS)?window.ML_COACH_TACTICS:[]}
function tacticCoachCurrent(){return tacticCoachPresets().find(x=>x.id===st.tacticPreset)||tacticCoachPresets()[0]}
function tacticsPage(){
 const rows=st.players;const coach=tacticCoachCurrent();
 const arrowPaths=st.tacticArrows.map(a=>{const x=rows[a.from],y=rows[a.to];if(!x||!y)return '';
  const kind=['pass','run','dribble'].includes(a.kind)?a.kind:'run';
  const color=kind==='pass'?'#e5fb77':kind==='dribble'?'#ffb986':'#72dbff';
  const dash=kind==='run'?'2 1.3':kind==='dribble'?'1 1':'';
  return `<line x1="${x.x}" y1="${x.y}" x2="${y.x}" y2="${y.y}" stroke="${color}" stroke-width=".8" stroke-dasharray="${dash}" marker-end="url(#tarrow-${kind})"/>`;}).join('');
 const drawings=(st.tacticSketch||[]).map(stroke=>`<polyline points="${stroke.map(q=>`${Number(q.x)||0},${Number(q.y)||0}`).join(' ')}" fill="none" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-width=".6" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round"/>`).join('');
 const sel=st.players[st.tacticSelected]||rows[0];
 const enemy=st.showOpponent?formPositions('4-4-2').map((q,i)=>`<span class="tactic-rival" title="Rəqib #${i+1}" style="left:${q.x}%;top:${100-q.y}%">${i+1}</span>`).join(''):'';
 const posMark=st.tacticPhase==='defence'?`<div class="tactic-block" style="top:${100-st.tacticLine}%;width:${st.tacticWidth}%;"></div>`:`<div class="tactic-block attack" style="top:${100-st.tacticLine}%;width:${st.tacticWidth}%;"></div>`;
 const sceneInfo=st.tacticScenes.length?`${st.tacticSceneIndex+1}/${st.tacticScenes.length}`:'Səhnə yoxdur';
 return `${title('TACTICAL STUDIO',T('tactics'),'Düzülüş, rəqib, məşqçi arxivi, pressinq, oxlar, mərhələli plan və görüntü ixracı. Mənbə ilə məxsusi məşqçi mövsümünü ayır.')}
 <div class="row" style="margin-bottom:16px"><span class="tag">11 oyunçu</span><span class="tag">${esc(st.formation)}</span><span class="tag">${st.tacticPhase==='attack'?'Hücum':'Müdafiə'}</span>${st.tacticDraw?'<span class="tag warn">İki oyunçu seçərək ox əlavə et</span>':''}${st.tacticFreeDraw?'<span class="tag warn">Meydan üzərinə çək</span>':''}</div>
 <div class="panel pad tactic-coach-panel"><div class="between"><h3>🎓 Mənbəli məşqçi taktikaları</h3><span class="tag">${tacticCoachPresets().length} tarixi nümunə</span></div><div class="formgrid"><label class="formfield">Məşqçi / klub / mövsüm<select id="coach-preset">${tacticCoachPresets().map(t=>`<option value="${esc(t.id)}" ${t.id===st.tacticPreset?'selected':''}>${esc(t.coach+' · '+t.club+' ('+t.season+')')}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><div class="formfield"><span>Əsas formasiya</span><div class="row"><span class="tag">${esc(coach?.formation||'—')}</span><span class="tag">Müdafiə: ${esc(coach?.defence||'—')}</span></div></div></div><p>${esc(coach?.philosophy||'')}</p><p class="footnote">${esc(coach?.instruction||'')} Nümunə konkret mövsümə aiddir, məşqçinin bütün oyunları üçün eyni taktika deyil.</p><div class="row"><button class="btn primary sm" data-tactic="coach-apply">Bu taktikaya keç</button>${coach?.source?`<a class="btn sm" href="${esc(coach.source)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Taktiki təhlilin mənbəsi ↗</a>`:''}</div></div>
 <div class="tactic-layout"><div class="pitchwrap"><div class="pitch" id="pitch"><div class="half"></div><div class="centre"></div><div class="area pitch-north"></div><div class="area pitch-south"></div><div class="goal pitch-north"></div><div class="goal pitch-south"></div>${posMark}${enemy}<svg class="pitch-arrows" preserveAspectRatio="none" viewBox="0 0 100 100"><defs>${[['pass','#e5fb77'],['run','#72dbff'],['dribble','#ffb986']].map(([k,c])=>`<marker id="tarrow-${k}" markerWidth="4" markerHeight="4" refX="3" refY="2" orient="auto"><path d="M0 0L4 2L0 4Z" fill="${c}"/></marker>`).join('')}</defs>${arrowPaths}${drawings}<polyline id="tactic-drawing-now" fill="none" stroke="white" stroke-width=".6" points=""/></svg>${rows.map((r,i)=>`<button type="button" class="player-dot ${st.tacticColor==='away'?'alt':''} ${st.tacticSelected===i?'selected':''}" data-player="${i}" style="left:${r.x}%;top:${r.y}%"><span class="jersey">${esc(r.num)}${safeImg(r.image)?`<img class="tactic-portrait" src="${esc(safeImg(r.image))}" alt="" loading="lazy" onerror="this.remove()">`:''}</span><span class="pname">${esc(r.name)}</span></button>`).join('')}</div><p class="footnote">Oyunçunu sürüşdür; ox rejimində iki oyunçunu seç; sərbəst çək rejimində meydança üzərinə çək. Rəqib simvolları məşq məqsədli təxmini 4-4-2 mövqeləridir.</p>
 <div class="tactic-scenes panel pad"><div class="between"><h3>🎞 Hərəkət mərhələləri</h3><span id="scene-status" class="tag">${esc(sceneInfo)}</span></div><p class="footnote">Oyunçuları yerləşdirib “Səhnə saxla” bas. İki və daha çox səhnə olduqda hərəkəti oynat.</p><div class="row"><button class="btn sm" data-tactic="scene-add">+ Səhnə saxla</button><button class="btn sm" data-tactic="scene-prev">‹</button><button class="btn sm" data-tactic="scene-next">›</button><button class="btn sm" data-tactic="scene-play" ${st.tacticScenes.length<2?'disabled':''}>▶ Oynat</button><button class="btn sm" data-tactic="scene-stop">■ Dayandır</button><button class="btn sm danger" data-tactic="scene-clear">Təmizlə</button></div></div></div>
 <div class="editor"><h3>Formasiya və taktika</h3><label class="formfield">Düzülüş<select id="formation">${FORMATIONS.map(x=>`<option value="${x}" ${st.formation===x?'selected':''}>${x}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><div class="formgrid"><label class="formfield">Oyun mərhələsi<select id="tactic-phase"><option value="attack" ${st.tacticPhase==='attack'?'selected':''}>Hücum</option><option value="defence" ${st.tacticPhase==='defence'?'selected':''}>Müdafiə</option></select></label><label class="formfield">Forma rəngi<select id="kit"><option value="home" ${st.tacticColor==='home'?'selected':''}>Yaşıl</option><option value="away" ${st.tacticColor==='away'?'selected':''}>Mavi</option></select></label></div>
 <label class="club-setting-toggle"><input id="tactic-opponent" type="checkbox" ${st.showOpponent?'checked':''}> <span>Rəqib 11-liyi göstər (təxmini)</span></label>
 ${[['tactic-press','Pressinq intensivliyi',st.tacticPress],['tactic-width','Komandanın eni',st.tacticWidth],['tactic-line','Müdafiə xəttinin hündürlüyü',st.tacticLine]].map(([id,label,v])=>`<label class="formfield">${esc(label)} · ${v}%<input type="range" id="${id}" min="10" max="90" step="5" value="${v}"></label>`).join('')}
 <div class="tactic-tools"><h3>Hərəkət və standart vəziyyətlər</h3><label class="formfield">Ox növü<select id="tactic-arrow-kind"><option value="run" ${st.tacticArrowKind==='run'?'selected':''}>Göy: qaçış</option><option value="pass" ${st.tacticArrowKind==='pass'?'selected':''}>Sarı: ötürmə</option><option value="dribble" ${st.tacticArrowKind==='dribble'?'selected':''}>Narıncı: driblinq</option></select></label><div class="row"><button class="btn sm ${st.tacticDraw?'active':''}" data-tactic="draw">↗ ${st.tacticDraw?'Ox bitir':'Ox əlavə et'}</button><button class="btn sm ${st.tacticFreeDraw?'active':''}" data-tactic="free-draw">✎ Sərbəst çək</button><button class="btn sm" data-tactic="clear-arrows">Oxları sil</button><button class="btn sm" data-tactic="clear-sketch">Cizgiləri sil</button><button class="btn sm" data-tactic="undo">↶ Geri al</button></div><div class="row"><button class="btn sm" data-tactic="corner">🚩 Künc zərbəsi</button><button class="btn sm" data-tactic="freekick">⚽ Cərimə zərbəsi</button></div></div>
 <div class="tactic-tools"><h3>Seçilmiş oyunçu #${st.tacticSelected+1}</h3><div class="formgrid"><label class="formfield">Ad<input id="tactic-name" maxlength="32" value="${esc(sel.name)}"></label><label class="formfield">Nömrə<input id="tactic-num" type="number" min="0" max="99" value="${esc(sel.num)}"></label></div><label class="formfield">Mövqe / rol<select id="tactic-role">${['GK','DEF','MID','ATT','CB','LB','RB','LWB','RWB','CDM','CM','CAM','LM','RM','LW','RW','CF','ST'].map(x=>`<option ${sel.role===x?'selected':''}>${x}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><button class="btn primary" data-tactic="apply">Dəyişiklikləri tətbiq et</button></div>
 <label class="formfield">Taktika qeydləri<textarea id="tactic-notes" maxlength="1500" placeholder="Press, müdafiə xətti, keçidlər…">${esc(st.tacticNotes)}</textarea></label><div class="row"><button class="btn primary" data-tactic="save">Yadda saxla</button><button class="btn" data-tactic="export">↓ JSON</button><button class="btn" data-tactic="export-png">↓ PNG</button><button class="btn" data-tactic="import">↑ İdxal</button><input type="file" id="import-file" accept="application/json,.json" style="display:none"><button class="btn danger" data-tactic="reset">Sıfırla</button></div><p class="footnote">Taktika, mərhələlər və parametrlər brauzerdə saxlanır. Məşqçi nümunələri izahlı şablonlardır; oyunçuların konkret heyəti uydurulmur.</p></div></div>`;
}
function attachProfileToTactic(){
 const source=st.profiles[st.selected]||MAP.get(st.selected);
 if(!source||(source.kind!=='players'&&!(source.roles||[]).includes('players')))return toast('Yalnız futbolçu profili seçilə bilər');
 // If the user has not selected a tactical slot, do not automatically put
 // a verified midfielder in the goalkeeper's default position.
 const position=norm(source.position||'');
 if(!st.tacticSelectionExplicit&&st.tacticSelected===0&&/(midfield|yarim|orta saha|centro|medio)/.test(position)){
  const middle=st.players.map((p,i)=>({p,i})).filter(x=>['MID','CM','CDM','CAM'].includes(x.p.role));
  if(middle.length)st.tacticSelected=middle.sort((a,b)=>Math.abs(a.p.x-50)-Math.abs(b.p.x-50))[0].i;
 }
 const target=st.players[st.tacticSelected];if(!target)return;
 tacticPush();target.name=String(source.name||'Oyunçu').slice(0,32);
 target.profileId=String(source.id||'').slice(0,40);
 target.image=photoFor(source)||''; // verified profile binding; never search by name
 tacticStore();st.page='tactics';st.tacticDraw=false;render();toast('Futbolçu taktikaya yerləşdirildi');
}
function tacticSnapshot(){return {formation:st.formation,players:structuredClone(st.players),arrows:structuredClone(st.tacticArrows),color:st.tacticColor,notes:st.tacticNotes,press:st.tacticPress,width:st.tacticWidth,line:st.tacticLine,phase:st.tacticPhase,opponent:st.showOpponent,sketch:structuredClone(st.tacticSketch),scenes:structuredClone(st.tacticScenes),setPiece:st.tacticSetPiece}}
function tacticPush(){st.tacticHistory.push(tacticSnapshot());if(st.tacticHistory.length>30)st.tacticHistory.shift()}
function tacticStore(){save('formation',st.formation);save('pitchPlayers',st.players);save('pitchArrows',st.tacticArrows);save('pitchNotes',st.tacticNotes);save('tacticColor',st.tacticColor);save('tacticPress',st.tacticPress);save('tacticWidth',st.tacticWidth);save('tacticLine',st.tacticLine);save('tacticPhase',st.tacticPhase);save('tacticOpponent',st.showOpponent);save('tacticSketch',st.tacticSketch);save('tacticScenes',st.tacticScenes);save('tacticSetPiece',st.tacticSetPiece);save('tacticPreset',st.tacticPreset)}
let tacticSceneTimer=null;
function stopTacticScenes(redraw=true){if(tacticSceneTimer){clearInterval(tacticSceneTimer);tacticSceneTimer=null}st.tacticPlayback=false;if(redraw&&st.page==='tactics')render()}
function applyTacticScene(index,quick=false){const s=st.tacticScenes[index];if(!s)return;st.tacticSceneIndex=index;if(s.players?.length===11){st.players=s.players.map(x=>({...x}));if(quick){for(let i=0;i<11;i++){const dot=document.querySelector(`#pitch [data-player="${i}"]`);if(dot){dot.style.left=st.players[i].x+'%';dot.style.top=st.players[i].y+'%'}}const lab=document.getElementById('scene-status');if(lab)lab.textContent=(index+1)+'/'+st.tacticScenes.length;}else render();}}
function exportTacticPNG(){const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=740;canvas.height=960;const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');if(!ctx){toast('Bu brauzer PNG dəstəkləmir');return}ctx.fillStyle='#16634b';ctx.fillRect(0,0,740,960);ctx.strokeStyle='#e5fbe8';ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.strokeRect(24,24,692,912);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(24,480);ctx.lineTo(716,480);ctx.stroke();ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(370,480,95,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();ctx.strokeRect(215,24,310,170);ctx.strokeRect(215,766,310,170);ctx.font='bold 16px sans-serif';for(const p of st.players){const x=24+692*p.x/100,y=24+912*p.y/100;ctx.beginPath();ctx.fillStyle=st.tacticColor==='away'?'#62c9f3':'#b5f568';ctx.arc(x,y,18,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.fillStyle='#13202a';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillText(String(p.num),x,y+5);ctx.fillStyle='white';ctx.font='13px sans-serif';ctx.fillText(String(p.name||'').slice(0,19),x,y+35);ctx.font='bold 16px sans-serif'}for(const a of st.tacticArrows){const p=st.players[a.from],q=st.players[a.to];if(!p||!q)continue;ctx.strokeStyle=a.kind==='pass'?'#e5fb77':a.kind==='dribble'?'#ffb986':'#72dbff';ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(24+692*p.x/100,24+912*p.y/100);ctx.lineTo(24+692*q.x/100,24+912*q.y/100);ctx.stroke()}const a=document.createElement('a');a.download='MATCHLAB_taktika.png';a.href=canvas.toDataURL('image/png');a.click();toast('PNG hazırlandı')}
function tacticAction(action){
 if(action==='scene-stop'){stopTacticScenes();return}
 if(tacticSceneTimer)stopTacticScenes(false);
 if(action==='apply'){tacticPush();let p=st.players[st.tacticSelected];p.name=(document.getElementById('tactic-name')?.value||p.name).trim().slice(0,32)||'Oyunçu';p.num=Math.min(99,Math.max(0,Number(document.getElementById('tactic-num')?.value)||0));p.role=document.getElementById('tactic-role')?.value||p.role;st.tacticNotes=document.getElementById('tactic-notes')?.value||'';tacticStore();render();toast('Oyunçu dəyişdi');return}
 if(action==='draw'){st.tacticDraw=!st.tacticDraw;st.tacticFreeDraw=false;st.tacticFrom=null;render();return}
 if(action==='free-draw'){st.tacticFreeDraw=!st.tacticFreeDraw;st.tacticDraw=false;st.tacticFrom=null;render();return}
 if(action==='coach-apply'){const t=tacticCoachCurrent();if(!t)return;tacticPush();st.formation=t.formation;st.players=freshPlayers(t.formation).map((v,i)=>({...st.players[i],...{x:v.x,y:v.y,role:v.role}}));st.tacticPress=t.press;st.tacticWidth=t.width;st.tacticLine=t.line;st.tacticNotes=t.coach+' · '+t.club+' · '+t.season+': '+t.instruction;st.tacticPhase='attack';st.tacticSetPiece='none';st.tacticArrows=[];st.tacticSketch=[];tacticStore();render();toast('Mənbəli formasiya tətbiq edildi');return}
 if(action==='scene-add'){if(st.tacticScenes.length>=8){toast('Ən çox 8 səhnə saxlanıla bilər');return}st.tacticScenes.push({players:st.players.map(x=>({...x})),name:'Səhnə '+(st.tacticScenes.length+1)});st.tacticSceneIndex=st.tacticScenes.length-1;tacticStore();render();return}
 if(action==='scene-prev'||action==='scene-next'){if(!st.tacticScenes.length)return toast('Əvvəl səhnə saxla');const dir=action==='scene-next'?1:-1;applyTacticScene((st.tacticSceneIndex+dir+st.tacticScenes.length)%st.tacticScenes.length);return}
 if(action==='scene-play'){if(st.tacticScenes.length<2)return toast('Ən azı iki səhnə əlavə et');st.tacticPlayback=true;let index=st.tacticSceneIndex;applyTacticScene(index,true);tacticSceneTimer=setInterval(()=>{if(st.page!=='tactics'||document.hidden){stopTacticScenes(false);return}index=(index+1)%st.tacticScenes.length;applyTacticScene(index,true)},1450);return}
 if(action==='scene-clear'){tacticPush();st.tacticScenes=[];st.tacticSceneIndex=0}
 if(action==='corner'||action==='freekick'){tacticPush();const corner=[[50,92],[20,72],[45,72],[75,72],[30,50],[55,52],[80,49],[18,25],[42,24],[67,20],[7,11]];const free=[[50,90],[20,70],[43,70],[75,70],[24,52],[50,48],[78,48],[28,30],[48,22],[67,26],[53,12]];const positions=action==='corner'?corner:free;st.players.forEach((player,i)=>{player.x=positions[i][0];player.y=positions[i][1]});st.tacticSetPiece=action;st.tacticNotes=(action==='corner'?'Künc zərbəsi':'Cərimə zərbəsi')+' üçün təlim şablonu; real klubun təsdiqlənmiş standart vəziyyəti deyil.';}
 if(action==='clear-arrows'){tacticPush();st.tacticArrows=[]}
 if(action==='clear-sketch'){tacticPush();st.tacticSketch=[]}
 if(action==='undo'){let prev=st.tacticHistory.pop();if(!prev){toast('Geri alınacaq dəyişiklik yoxdur');return}Object.assign(st,{formation:prev.formation,players:prev.players,tacticArrows:prev.arrows,tacticColor:prev.color,tacticNotes:prev.notes,tacticPress:prev.press??65,tacticWidth:prev.width??60,tacticLine:prev.line??65,tacticPhase:prev.phase||'attack',showOpponent:!!prev.opponent,tacticSketch:prev.sketch||[],tacticScenes:prev.scenes||[],tacticSetPiece:prev.setPiece||'none'});}
 if(action==='save'){st.tacticNotes=document.getElementById('tactic-notes')?.value??st.tacticNotes;tacticStore();toast('Taktika saxlandı')}
 if(action==='export'){st.tacticNotes=document.getElementById('tactic-notes')?.value??st.tacticNotes;const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify({version:2,...tacticSnapshot()},null,2)],{type:'application/json'});const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);let a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='MATCHLAB_taktika.json';a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1500);return}
 if(action==='export-png'){exportTacticPNG();return}
 if(action==='import'){document.getElementById('import-file')?.click();return}
 if(action==='reset'){tacticPush();st.players=freshPlayers(st.formation);st.tacticArrows=[];st.tacticSketch=[];st.tacticNotes='';st.tacticSelected=0;st.tacticSetPiece='none'}
 tacticStore();render();}

function isValidTactic(x){return x&&FORMATIONS.includes(x.formation)&&Array.isArray(x.players)&&x.players.length===11&&x.players.every(p=>typeof p.name==='string'&&p.name.length<=32&&Number.isFinite(p.x)&&Number.isFinite(p.y)&&p.x>=0&&p.x<=100&&p.y>=0&&p.y<=100&&Number.isFinite(Number(p.num)))&&Array.isArray(x.arrows)&&x.arrows.length<100&&x.arrows.every(a=>Number.isInteger(a.from)&&Number.isInteger(a.to)&&a.from>=0&&a.to>=0&&a.from<11&&a.to<11)}
async function importTactic(file){try{const x=JSON.parse((await file.text()).slice(0,150000));if(!isValidTactic(x))throw Error('Format');tacticPush();st.formation=x.formation;st.players=x.players.map(p=>({x:p.x,y:p.y,name:p.name.slice(0,32),num:Math.max(0,Math.min(99,Number(p.num))),role:String(p.role||'CM').slice(0,7),profileId:String(p.profileId||'').slice(0,40),image:safeImg(p.image)}));st.tacticArrows=x.arrows.map(a=>({...a,kind:['run','pass','dribble'].includes(a.kind)?a.kind:'run'}));st.tacticColor=x.color==='away'?'away':'home';st.tacticNotes=String(x.notes||'').slice(0,1500);st.tacticPress=Math.max(10,Math.min(90,Number(x.press)||65));st.tacticWidth=Math.max(10,Math.min(90,Number(x.width)||60));st.tacticLine=Math.max(10,Math.min(90,Number(x.line)||65));st.tacticPhase=x.phase==='defence'?'defence':'attack';st.showOpponent=!!x.opponent;st.tacticSketch=Array.isArray(x.sketch)?x.sketch.slice(0,50).filter(s=>Array.isArray(s)&&s.length<500):[];st.tacticScenes=Array.isArray(x.scenes)?x.scenes.slice(0,8).filter(s=>Array.isArray(s.players)&&s.players.length===11&&s.players.every(p=>Number.isFinite(p.x)&&Number.isFinite(p.y)&&p.x>=0&&p.x<=100&&p.y>=0&&p.y<=100)):[];tacticStore();render();toast('Taktika idxal edildi')}catch{toast('JSON taktika faylı düzgün deyil')}}
function usePlayer(i){st.tacticSelectionExplicit=true;if(st.tacticDraw){if(st.tacticFrom==null){st.tacticFrom=i;st.tacticSelected=i;toast('Oxun son oyunçusunu seç')}else if(st.tacticFrom!==i){tacticPush();st.tacticArrows.push({from:st.tacticFrom,to:i,kind:st.tacticArrowKind});st.tacticFrom=null;tacticStore();toast('Qaçış oxu əlavə edildi')}render();return}st.tacticSelected=i;render()}
let drawingStroke=null;
function pointOnPitch(e,element){const rect=element.getBoundingClientRect();return {x:Math.round(Math.max(0,Math.min(100,(e.clientX-rect.left)/rect.width*100))*10)/10,y:Math.round(Math.max(0,Math.min(100,(e.clientY-rect.top)/rect.height*100))*10)/10}}
root.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{const pitch=e.target.closest('#pitch');if(!pitch||!st.tacticFreeDraw||e.target.closest('[data-player]'))return;e.preventDefault();if(st.tacticSketch.length>=50)return toast('Maksimum 50 sərbəst xətt');drawingStroke=[pointOnPitch(e,pitch)];pitch.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId)});
root.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(!drawingStroke)return;const pitch=document.getElementById('pitch');if(!pitch)return;drawingStroke.push(pointOnPitch(e,pitch));if(drawingStroke.length>400)drawingStroke=drawingStroke.slice(0,400);pitch.querySelector('#tactic-drawing-now')?.setAttribute('points',drawingStroke.map(q=>q.x+','+q.y).join(' '))});
root.addEventListener('pointerup',()=>{if(!drawingStroke)return;if(drawingStroke.length>=2){tacticPush();st.tacticSketch.push(drawingStroke);tacticStore()}drawingStroke=null;render()});
let dragging=null;
root.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{const b=e.target.closest('[data-player]');if(!b||st.page!=='tactics'||st.tacticDraw)return;const i=Number(b.dataset.player);if(!Number.isInteger(i))return;dragging={i,x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY,moved:false};b.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);});
root.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(!dragging||st.page!=='tactics')return;const el=document.getElementById('pitch');if(!el)return;if(Math.hypot(e.clientX-dragging.x,e.clientY-dragging.y)<5&&!dragging.moved)return;if(!dragging.moved){tacticPush();dragging.moved=true}const rect=el.getBoundingClientRect();const p=st.players[dragging.i];p.x=Math.round(Math.max(6,Math.min(94,((e.clientX-rect.left)/rect.width)*100))*10)/10;p.y=Math.round(Math.max(5,Math.min(95,((e.clientY-rect.top)/rect.height)*100))*10)/10;const dot=el.querySelector(`[data-player="${dragging.i}"]`);if(dot){dot.style.left=p.x+'%';dot.style.top=p.y+'%'}});
root.addEventListener('pointerup',e=>{if(!dragging)return;const i=dragging.i,moved=dragging.moved;dragging=null;if(moved){st.tacticSelected=i;tacticStore();render();toast('Mövqe dəyişdi')}else usePlayer(i)});
async function browserPhoto(p){
 if(!navigator.onLine)return {url:''};
 const qid=p.wikidataId||p.qid||(p.id.startsWith('Q')?p.id:'');
 if(/^Q[1-9]\d{0,11}$/.test(qid)){
  const url='https://www.wikidata.org/w/api.php?action=wbgetentities&ids='+encodeURIComponent(qid)+'&props=claims&format=json&origin=*';
  const r=await req(url,6500);const entity=r.entities?.[qid];
  const prop=p.kind==='teams'?'P154':'P18';
  const filename=entity?.claims?.[prop]?.find(x=>x.mainsnak?.snaktype==='value')?.mainsnak?.datavalue?.value;
  if(typeof filename==='string'&&filename.length<200)return {url:'https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:FilePath/'+encodeURIComponent(filename)+'?width=280'};
 }
 // For ambiguous or unknown people, avoid matching an unrelated Wikipedia photo by name.
 return {url:''};
}
const photoMisses=new Map();
function photoMissed(id){const expiry=photoMisses.get(id);if(!expiry)return false;if(Date.now()>expiry){photoMisses.delete(id);return false}return true}
function markPhotoMiss(id){photoMisses.set(id,Date.now()+10*60*1000)}
// On-demand enrichment: every visible record with a verified QID is eligible.
// Never mark records outside the current batch as missing.
async function enrichVisibleMedia(){
 const featured=st.page==='home' ? ['Q5705','Q11571','Q21621995','Q28967995','Q8682','Q7156','Q723476','Q9617','p:pel','p:diego-maradona','p:johan-cruyff','p:franz-beckenbauer','c:pep-guardiola'].map(id=>st.profiles[id]||MAP.get(id)).filter(Boolean):[];
 const candidates=(st.page==='home'?featured:st.items.slice(0,75)).filter(p=>p&&!photoFor(p)&&!photoMissed(p.id)&&!st.photoLoading.has(p.id)&&personQid(p));
 if(!candidates.length)return;
 if(!st.backend){for(const p of candidates.slice(0,7))maybePhoto(p);return}
 const generation=st.searchGeneration;
 for(const kind of ['players','teams','coaches']){
  const eligible=candidates.filter(p=>p.kind===kind);
  // Wikidata supports batch IDs; our own server limits each request to 20 IDs.
  for(let offset=0;offset<eligible.length;offset+=20){
   if(st.page!=='home'&&generation!==st.searchGeneration)return;
   const batch=eligible.slice(offset,offset+20);
   const qids=[...new Set(batch.map(personQid))];
   for(const p of batch)st.photoLoading.add(p.id);
   try{
    const r=await req('/api/photos?qids='+encodeURIComponent(qids.join(','))+'&kind='+kind,16000);
    let changed=false;
    for(const p of batch){const url=safeImg(r.items?.[personQid(p)]);
     if(url&&!brokenPhotos.has(url)){st.photos[p.id]=url;const meta=r.details?.[personQid(p)];if(meta)st.photoMeta[p.id]=meta;changed=true}
     else if(r.complete!==false)markPhotoMiss(p.id);
    }
    if(changed){save('photoCache',st.photos);save('photoMeta',st.photoMeta);if(['search','home'].includes(st.page))render()}
   }catch{} // Network failures are not evidence that the image does not exist.
   finally{for(const p of batch)st.photoLoading.delete(p.id)}
  }
 }
}
function maybePhoto(p,retry=false){
 if(!p||(!retry&&photoFor(p))||st.photoLoading.has(p.id)||(!retry&&photoMissed(p.id)))return;
 const qid=p.wikidataId||p.qid||(p.id.startsWith('Q')?p.id:'');
 if(!/^Q[1-9]\d{0,11}$/.test(qid))return;
 st.photoLoading.add(p.id);
 const task=st.backend?req('/api/photo?qid='+encodeURIComponent(qid)+'&kind='+encodeURIComponent(p.kind)+(retry?'&retry=1':''),17000):browserPhoto(p);
 task.then(r=>{if(safeImg(r.url)&&!brokenPhotos.has(safeImg(r.url))){st.photos[p.id]=r.url;st.photoMeta[p.id]=r;save('photoCache',st.photos);save('photoMeta',st.photoMeta);if(['profile','compare','home','search'].includes(st.page))render()}else markPhotoMiss(p.id)})
 .catch(()=>{}).finally(()=>st.photoLoading.delete(p.id));
}
let backendProbe=null;
async function detectBackend(){
 if(!BACKEND()){st.connChecked=true;st.connecting=false;return false}
 if(backendProbe)return backendProbe;
 st.connecting=true;
 if(!st.backend)render();
 backendProbe=(async()=>{
  const wasConnected=st.backend;
  try{
   // Free hosts can be cold. Do not mark the application permanently offline
   // on a single short timeout: retry in the background and on page visibility.
   const r=await req('/api/status',9500);
   if(!r.backend||r.ok===false)throw new Error('Backend status unavailable');
   st.backend=true;st.publicHosting=Boolean(r.publicHosting);st.lastHealthy=Date.now();st.connectionError='';
   st.world=r.world||null;
   st.install={state:st.world?.total?'ready':'idle',message:st.world?.total?'Dünya bazası hazırdır':'Dünya bazası hələ quraşdırılmayıb'};
   if(st.world?.total&&(!st.countries||!st.countries.length)){
     try{const data=await req('/api/world/countries',5500);st.countries=data.items||[]}catch{}
   }
   st.connChecked=true;st.connecting=false;render();
   if(!wasConnected){
    if(st.page==='search'&&st.world?.total)search();
    if(['matches','home','match'].includes(st.page))loadLive();
    if(st.page==='home')enrichVisibleMedia();
   }
   return true;
  }catch(e){
   st.backend=false;st.publicHosting=false;st.connectionError=e?.name==='AbortError'?'Server gec cavab verir — yenidən yoxlanacaq.':'Server əlçatan deyil — yenidən yoxlanacaq.';
   st.connChecked=true;st.connecting=false;render();
   return false;
  }finally{backendProbe=null}
 })();
 return backendProbe;
}

function shiftDate(amount){const d=new Date(st.date+'T12:00:00Z');d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate()+amount);st.date=d.toISOString().slice(0,10);loadLive()}
function requestNotify(){
 st.notifyConfig.enabled=true;save('notifyConfig',st.notifyConfig);
 if(!('Notification' in window)||!isSecureContext){toast('Tətbiqdaxili xəbərdarlıq aktivdir; sistem bildirişləri HTTPS/localhost və brauzer icazəsi tələb edir');render();return}

}
// No user-visible notification history is stored. Legacy history is cleared on load.
try{localStorage.removeItem(KEY+'alerts')}catch{}
const prior=new Map(),maxNotified=new Map(),detailObserved=new Map();
const notifiedHistory=new Set(read('notifiedKeys',[]));
function notifyWatch(m){if(st.notifyConfig?.scope==='all')return true;
 const f=Array.isArray(st.favs)?st.favs:[];
 if(f.some(x=>x.kind==='match'&&x.id===m.id))return true;
 if(Array.isArray(st.starredLeagues)&&st.starredLeagues.includes(m.league))return true;
 return f.some(x=>x.kind==='teams'&&[m.home?.name,m.away?.name].some(n=>norm(n)===norm(x.name)));
}
// Unique, original MATCHLAB tones. Each category differs in rhythm, pitch and timbre.
// Notes are [delaySeconds, frequencyHz, durationSeconds, oscillatorType, loudness, glideToHz?].
const NOTIFY_SOUND_LABELS=[
 ['goal','⚽ Qol','Stadion fanfarı · yüksələn, şən melodiya'],
 ['kickoff','▶ Başlanğıc','Hakim fiti · iki yüksələn siqnal'],
 ['final','🏁 Matç bitdi','Final fiti · üç uzun siqnal'],
 ['yellow','🟨 Sarı kart','Diqqət · iki qısa, parlaq işarə'],
 ['red','🟥 Qırmızı kart','Ciddi xəbərdarlıq · aşağı tezlikli həyəcan'],
 ['sub','🔄 Əvəzetmə','Dəyişiklik · yuxarı-aşağı yumşaq zəng'],
 ['var','📺 VAR','Video yoxlaması · rəqəmsal, fasiləli siqnal']
];
const NOTIFY_SOUND_PATTERNS=Object.freeze({
 goal:[[0,392,.14,'triangle',.7],[.11,494,.14,'triangle',.7],[.22,587,.16,'triangle',.7],[.34,784,.24,'triangle',.8],[.62,587,.13,'sine',.45],[.73,784,.29,'triangle',.75],[.73,988,.29,'sine',.28]],
 kickoff:[[0,1050,.18,'sine',.7,1340],[.29,1140,.28,'sine',.75,1510]],
 final:[[0,740,.30,'triangle',.58],[.38,660,.32,'triangle',.62],[.80,520,.46,'triangle',.65]],
 yellow:[[0,1050,.072,'sine',.6],[.16,1280,.10,'sine',.55]],
 red:[[0,270,.20,'sawtooth',.50,180],[.26,250,.20,'sawtooth',.55,165],[.53,230,.34,'sawtooth',.56,140]],
 sub:[[0,523,.13,'sine',.55],[.13,659,.13,'sine',.55],[.29,784,.14,'triangle',.5],[.47,659,.18,'sine',.48]],
 var:[[0,440,.09,'square',.28],[.16,440,.09,'square',.28],[.32,520,.10,'square',.28],[.57,620,.21,'sine',.48,780]]
});
let notifyAudioContext=null;
function soundNotification(category='goal',preview=false){
 if(!preview&&!st.notifyConfig.sound)return false;
 const volume=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(st.notifyConfig.volume??55)))/100;
 if(volume===0)return false;
 const pattern=NOTIFY_SOUND_PATTERNS[category];if(!pattern)return false;
 try{
  const AC=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(!AC)return false;
  if(!notifyAudioContext||notifyAudioContext.state==='closed')notifyAudioContext=new AC();
  const context=notifyAudioContext;
  const play=()=>{
   if(context.state!=='running')return;
   const when=context.currentTime+.02,master=.14*volume;
   for(const [delay,hz,seconds,wave,loudness,glide] of pattern){
    const osc=context.createOscillator(),gain=context.createGain(),begin=when+delay,end=begin+seconds;
    osc.type=wave;osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(hz,begin);
    if(glide)osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(glide,end);
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001,begin);
    gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(.0002,master*loudness),begin+Math.min(.025,seconds*.25));
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(Math.max(.0002,master*loudness),begin+seconds*.60);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,end);
    osc.connect(gain);gain.connect(context.destination);
    osc.start(begin);osc.stop(end+.003);
    osc.onended=()=>{try{osc.disconnect();gain.disconnect()}catch{}};
   }
  };
  if(context.state==='suspended')context.resume().then(play).catch(()=>{if(preview)toast('Brauzer səsə icazə vermədi')});
  else play();
  return true;
 }catch{if(preview)toast('Brauzer bu səsi ifa edə bilmədi');return false}
}
function notifyEmit(key,title,message,m,category='goal'){
 if((!st.notifyConfig?.enabled&&!key.startsWith('test:'))||notifiedHistory.has(key)||!(notifyWatch(m)||key.startsWith('test:')))return;
 notifiedHistory.add(key);const keys=[...notifiedHistory].slice(-250);if(notifiedHistory.size>300){notifiedHistory.clear();keys.forEach(k=>notifiedHistory.add(k))}save('notifiedKeys',keys);
 toast(title+' · '+message);soundNotification(category);
 if('Notification' in window&&isSecureContext&&Notification.permission==='granted'){try{new Notification('MATCHLAB · '+title,{body:message,tag:key})}catch{}}
}
function applyNotifications(items){
 if(!Array.isArray(items))return;
 for(const m of items){if(!m?.id||!m.home||!m.away||m.stale)continue;
  const old=prior.get(m.id),high=maxNotified.get(m.id)||{homeScore:old?.homeScore??0,awayScore:old?.awayScore??0};
  // First sighting is a baseline: no notification for goals that happened before the tab opened.
  if(old&&m.state==='live'){
   if(st.notifyConfig.goal){for(const side of ['home','away']){const prev=old[side+'Score'],next=m[side+'Score'];if(Number.isInteger(prev)&&Number.isInteger(next)&&next>prev&&next>high[side+'Score'])notifyEmit(m.id+':goal:'+side+':'+next,'⚽ QOL!',m[side].name+' '+score(m),m,'goal')}}
   if(st.notifyConfig.kickoff&&old.state==='scheduled')notifyEmit(m.id+':kickoff','▶ Matç başladı',m.home.name+' — '+m.away.name,m,'kickoff');
  }
  if(old&&st.notifyConfig.final&&old.state==='live'&&m.state==='final')notifyEmit(m.id+':final','🏁 Matç bitdi',m.home.name+' — '+m.away.name+' '+score(m),m,'final');
  maxNotified.set(m.id,{homeScore:Math.max(high.homeScore||0,m.homeScore??0),awayScore:Math.max(high.awayScore||0,m.awayScore??0)});
  prior.set(m.id,m);
 }
}
function detailCategory(text){const v=String(text||'').toLowerCase();
 if(/\bvar\b|video assistant referee|video review/.test(v))return 'var';
 if(/red card|qırmızı kart|kırmızı kart|carton rouge|tarjeta roja/.test(v))return 'red';
 if(/yellow card|sarı kart|carton jaune|tarjeta amarilla/.test(v))return 'yellow';
 if(/substitution|substitui|substituição|substitución|əvəzetmə|oyuncu değişikliği/.test(v))return 'sub';
 return '';
}
function notifyMatchDetails(m,info){const events=info?.events;if(!m?.id||!Array.isArray(events)||m.state!=='live')return;
 const seen=new Set(events.map(e=>String(e.clock||'')+'|'+String(e.text||'')));
 const old=detailObserved.get(m.id);detailObserved.set(m.id,seen);if(!old)return;
 for(const e of events){const key=String(e.clock||'')+'|'+String(e.text||'');if(old.has(key))continue;const category=detailCategory(e.text);if(!category||!st.notifyConfig[category])continue;
  const labels={red:'🟥 Qırmızı kart',yellow:'🟨 Sarı kart',sub:'🔄 Əvəzetmə',var:'📺 VAR'};
  notifyEmit(m.id+':event:'+key,labels[category],(e.clock||'')+' '+String(e.text||'').slice(0,140),m,category);
 }
}
